## ⌗⌗⌗ CURRENCY, r2376+c54.155 — *what this document does NOT cover, stated because the grain gate caught it 22 revisions behind*

**⚑ THE PLAN BELOW STOPS AT c54.137 AND EIGHTEEN REVISIONS HAVE LANDED SINCE.** *Read this head before the
body; the body's picture of the collapse-perturbation arc is superseded in two places and its picture of the
programme's INSTRUMENTS is superseded entirely.*

**⌗ THE PHYSICS THAT MOVED (c54.138–c54.150).** *The tensor sector runs the transfer with no idealisation and
there are **no primordial $B$-modes** at $\sim5\times10^{-111}$; the leading-order interior is **adequate**,
with the Bianchi shear identified as the long-wavelength growing tensor mode; the true hydrodynamical scalar
variable is $z_S=a(a+4B/3A)/a'$ and the **scalar monodromy is $4\pi/\rho$, twice the tensor's**; recollapse
**is** the Nariai threshold; the composition is **derived** rather than bounded, $\rho\simeq5.4\times10^{-2}$,
with the progenitor turning around at $z\simeq1.5$ with a mass $4.3\times10^{52}$ kg.* ⚠ **And the full-lap
Floquet apparatus of c54.143–148, together with c54.149's own ground for withdrawing it, is WITHDRAWN.** *What
those revisions bracket is a question the corpus had never posed: **the construction's recursion** — for a mode
observed today, in whose harmonic basis does its history run, through how many passages, with which monodromy
at each. That was front #1, and it was a READING task, not a computation.* ✔✔✔ **SETTLED c54.162, by reading,
with one line of asymptotics where a computation was genuinely owed: THE RECURSION DOES NOT RUN ON MODES.**
*No spacelike datum crosses, so there is no patch/ambient harmonic map to be fixed or deferred — which is a
**stronger** verdict against the withdrawn apparatus than the withdrawal itself entered. Every mode in
$\ell\simeq28$–$2475$ arrives frozen, so the sub-patch question is moot. Frozen content has no phase, so no
mode label survives for a previous monodromy to act on. **The structure is one lap, not a tower; the recursion
is a genealogy of universes.*** ⚠ **And it removes an advertised prediction** — P7's "a cold species and no
other" does not hold; the crossing is lossless for every species. *A loss, recorded as one.*

**⌗⌗ AND THE PART THIS DOCUMENT HAS NO PICTURE OF AT ALL: the reproducibility layer failed an audit.**
*c54.151–155 ran a receipt-vs-sentence pass — is the computation cited a computation of the claim made? — and
the answers were 14/28 on the perturbation sector, **1/16** on the receipts that are cited and contain no

## ⌗⌗ THE COLLAPSE-PERTURBATION ARC, PROPAGATED (r2376+c54.118–c54.137) — *twenty revisions of work that reached the register and had not reached this file*

**⚑ WHAT THE ARC DID.** *It took the one question the corpus had never been able to pose sharply — what does the
progenitor put across the branch point, and what does the crossing do to it — and turned it into a computation
with a background, a connection, a monodromy and a normalisation. **Nine fronts closed.***

**⌗ THE PHYSICS, IN THE ORDER IT CAME.** *The flat bundle cannot carry a force (c54.120) and the compact face
supplies no second scale (c54.128), which together gave the **one-constant theorem**: a dimensionless magnitude
needs two invariants and the substrate has one. $\hat\Gamma$ is bounded below (c54.129). **The collapse leg is
scale-invariant** — it is the second root of $\beta(\beta-1)=2$, the matter-contraction dual the corpus had
denied having (c54.122) — but the vacuum-leg continuation is **diagonal**, and the evenness that forbids mixing
*is* the time symmetry (c54.123–124). **What supplies the odd part is radiation** (c54.130), which makes the
mixing generic and discontinuous at zero, with off-diagonal $2\pi/\rho$ (c54.131). The interior-to-observed mode
map is built and validated against P15's own low-$\ell$ deficit (c54.133). **Recollapse *is* the Nariai
threshold**, from a closed dust ball rather than the horizon cubic (c54.135). $\hbar$ is multiplicative in
$P(k)$, so it survives in $A_s$ and cancels in $n_s$ (c54.136). **And $F$ is computed (c54.137).**

**⇒⇒ THE ARC'S TERMINAL RESULT, AND IT IS A NEGATIVE ONE WORTH MORE THAN THE POSITIVE IT REPLACED.** *The
transfer law is $\mathcal{P}=(9/2)k^3D_k^2/(M^2\rho^6)$, exactly scale-invariant on vacuum data and carrying
**no fixed normalisation**. So the crossing determines how much an amplitude is magnified and not the amplitude
— **the one-constant theorem read at the level of the perturbations rather than of the couplings.** On the
progenitor's own vacuum it returns $3\times10^{-103}$ against an observed $2\times10^{-9}$: short by $10^{94}$,
**so the primordial statistics are classical and non-vacuum for the source that actually supplies them**, which
P15 had established only for the substrate's vacuum — the one vacuum the inheritance story was not claiming.*

**⇒ AND ONE POSITIVE THING THE ARC LEAVES: the progenitor's composition is bracketed on both sides** —
$2.6\times10^{-6}\le\rho\le1.1\times10^{-3}$ — *the child's spectrum from above, the parent's own
nucleosynthesis from below. **Two observables on opposite legs of one excursion, neither available to the other
until they were compared.***

**⚠⚠ AND THE ARC'S METHODOLOGICAL YIELD, which is the reason this block exists.** *Four live open problems and
cluster J's tenth germ **had never been registered at all**, and were found by asking a *paper's* frontier list
to name a lead per item — the papers and the register are independent indices and had never been compared. Two
more fronts (`L-172`, `L-173`) were sitting inside `L-150`'s text, invisible to every gate. ***A row that
absorbs eight revisions of work stops being a container and becomes a hiding place.***

## ⌗⌗ THE LANDING AUDIT (r2376+c54.86–c54.88) — *asked by Daryl: has P7's frontier SHRUNK, and did what left it FEED the synthesis at weight?*

**⚑ THE ANSWER: it shrank in substance and not on the page; what left it reached the synthesis in two places
and not the third; and the audit turned up the same defect the c54.60 sweep was run to fix, in the places that
sweep did not look — and then in a whole layer below them.**

**⌗ THE FRONTIER.** *Seven items, now six.* Item 7 was resolved in its own title and still listed, and is
**retired** below the list into a record of what closed; items 3 and 4 say inside themselves that the bulk of
their content is settled; item 5 had been **overtaken twice over** and did not say so. *The section now opens
by stating its own count and where each item stands* — because a frontier list whose entries quietly empty is
worse than none: a reader uses it to choose what to work.

**⚠⚠ THE DEFECT, AND IT IS THE SAME ONE THREE TIMES.**
1. ***P14's ABSTRACT asserted the identification P14's own section is titled after DENYING*** — "Which three the
   generations are seated on, and why it is not the hinges" — for fifty-five revisions.
2. ***P14's ABSTRACT ALSO PREDATED ITS OWN CENTRAL RESULT***: the whole colour arc appears nowhere in it. **A
   reader of the abstract would not learn that the paper reaches $SU(3)$.** *A fourth result is now stated
   there, with its negative half beside it — the bundle is FLAT, so the discrete content of colour and no force.*
3. ***P7's frontier item 5 stated the revision and then, three sentences later, restated the withdrawn reading***
   — correction and error adjacent, so the paragraph contradicted itself.
4. ***P7's synthesis named only the COMPACT-FACE route to $\su(3)$*** — the corpus's only route until c54.42.
   **Both routes now carried, with the difference stated: the compact face is where a continuous algebra with a
   curvature could sit; the Lorentzian route gives a flat bundle — structure, not coupling.**
5. ***And then the same claim was found live in the REGISTER layer*** — five forward-facing standing documents,
   including this one's siblings and the open-problems ledger's own family 6, whose ESTABLISHED line also
   predated the colour arc.

**⌗⌗ THE PATTERN, WHICH IS THE POINT AND IS NOW A BASE-RATE ENTRY.** ***A result lands in the section that
produced it and does not reach the places that SUMMARISE*** — abstracts, frontier lists, synthesis sections,
standing registers. **Those are exactly where a reader goes FIRST and exactly where a working node edits LAST**,
because the working node is inside the argument and the summary is outside it.

**⇒ `check_withdrawn.py`, THE NINTH GATE**, with two scopes: the papers, and the forward-facing standing
documents (nine exempt **by name** as historical records, since rewriting a fork record destroys the record).
*A withdrawn claim's wording may appear — the corpus quotes what it withdrew, and should — but only with a
revision marker within 900 characters.* **Verified against the pre-fix tree: `rc=1`, naming all four paper
occurrences.** ***And the standing order it creates: a withdrawal is not finished until its phrase is in that
registry.***

**⇒ AND, AT c54.103, A TENTH CHECK — `_caption_only_corrections()` inside `check_compile`, FOR THE SAME
PATTERN ONE FLOOR LOWER.** *The low-multipole depth was corrected at r2204 — new numbers, a new qualitative
feature (the deficit's minimum at $\ell=4$), and an explicit **"is superseded"** — **inside a figure caption,
and nowhere else**. For a hundred and seventy revisions the body, abstract, results list, `sec:scope` (twice)
and summary went on stating the superseded figure.* ***A caption is the one location in a paper that is
neither argument nor summary: no gate read it as prose, and no reader reaches it before the sentence it
contradicts.*** **The check FAILS on any caption carrying correction language whose decimal figures the body
does not also carry. Verified against the pre-fix tree: it fires and names all eleven orphaned numbers.**
*The fix was not to the gate but to the paper: the caption's three trailing paragraphs — the located
adiabaticity caveat, the exact transfer-matrix transmission, and the supersession — are now body prose, where
they were always owed.*

**⇒ AND `check_citations.py`, THE TENTH GATE, AT c54.106 — in `check_supersession`'s REPORT mould.**
*`H·2` asked for a corpus-wide citation read-through; a read-through is the wrong instrument, because
'does this named classical object carry an attribution WHERE IT IS USED' is a proximity question over a
fixed vocabulary.* **It never fails on content — three kinds of bare use appear and only one is a defect —
and it makes the item's 'not a reference dump' constraint STRUCTURAL by reporting per-paper FIRST USE, so
a paper is asked for one attribution per named result and never for more.** ⚠⚠ *It found fifteen

**⚑ AND WHAT DID NOT LAND IS NOW REGISTERED AS `L-135`, the largest landing debt in the corpus: the
WINDING/CHARGE SECTOR — banked in four receipts and carried by no paper.** *`P03_winding_and_closure` is where

material belong in P3, whose lap and graze points supply it, or in P14, whose matter content uses it?**

**⇒⇒ AND A THIRD DEBT, AT c54.115–c54.116, WHICH IS THE SAME SHAPE ONE LEVEL ABOVE THE PAPERS: THE FOLD
ITSELF LEAKED.** *`OPEN_PROBLEMS_MAP` states at its head that **every live item** was folded into the lead

those four were open work that NO GATE COULD SEE: not `check_burndown`, not `check_supersession`, not

**⌗ AND BOTH WERE FOUND THE SAME WAY, WHICH IS THE PART WORTH KEEPING: by asking a PAPER's frontier list to
name a lead for each of its items.** *`P07_frontier_currency` parses P7 `sec:frontiers` and maps each item to

checked against each other before.*** **The standing order: the map's header claim is now CHECKED rather than
asserted, by `computations/frontier_audit/L165_the_quantum_completion_was_never_folded.py`, and P7's frontier
is checked against the register by `P07_frontier_currency`. Both are cheap and both re-run.**

**⌗ AND THE `B·1` OPENING PAID FOR ITSELF INSIDE ONE PARAGRAPH OF P10.** *The coupled sector asserts the
boundary operator $\hat\Gamma$ 'bounded below by $\gamma$' where it builds the straddle, and lists 'whether
it is bounded below' among what is **open** four hundred characters later.* **Both are right of different
operators — the leading-order sum of squares and the complete interacting one — and the qualification was
missing.** ***The repair costs the argument nothing, which is why it is a qualification and not a retraction:
the direct-integral decomposition uses only that the spectrum MEET BOTH SIDES of $\tfrac34$, and a spectral
projection does not ask for a floor.*** *Receipt `P10_the_straddle_does_not_need_a_floor`.*

## ⌗⌗ THE LEADS SWEEP, PROPAGATED (r2376+c54.63–c54.84) — the register runs to two, and the seventh and eighth gates did most of the finding

**⚑ WHAT THE SWEEP PRODUCED.** *The lead register stood at 127 registered / 20 open when the colour arc closed.
It now stands at **127 registered · 125 struck · 2 open · 0 HOT**, and **almost nothing in it needed new
geometry** — the leads were answered by results already banked and never claimed against them.*

**⌗ THE RESULTS WORTH CARRYING FORWARD, in the order they matter:**

- ***THE TRANSVERSE PLANE IS THE $A_2$ WEIGHT PLANE.*** *The three vantages' signed areal radii are three linear
  forms; their zeros were read at c54.52 and **their mutual angles never were**. Taken: equal length, $120°$
  apart, sum zero, mutual cosine $-\tfrac12$ — **the weight system of the fundamental, reached from the metric
  geometry with no representation theory in the derivation.*** **So the $\mathbb{Z}_3$-fixed centre carries no
  wall because it is the ORIGIN of the weight plane and $\mathbf 3$ has no zero weight** — a theorem where the
  corpus had an observation, and it disposes of two standing leads that wanted a state seated there.
- ***AT ODD $D$ THE HORIZON POLYNOMIAL IS EVEN IN $r$, AND FOUR SEPARATE FAILURES ARE THAT ONE FACT:*** the mass
  function's evenness (no $\gamma^5$); $-r_0$ lying in the geometry's **own** root set, so the parity has
  nothing to relate; the monodromy commuting with a fixed-point-free involution, so it cannot be $S_{D-1}$; and
  the root set being $\pm$-paired, so it is not $A_2$. **Three of the four were reached in three different
  revisions for three different leads and none named the evenness.**
- ***$\lambda^2/\Omega_c^2 = D-3$ EXACTLY, for every $M$ and $\alpha$.*** **So the eikonal quality factor's
  mass-independence was never a four-dimensional fact — it holds in every dimension. What is four-dimensional
  is the VALUE being one.** *`O6`'s scope was recorded the other way round.*
- ***COLOUR IS A SPACELIKE RELATION AND ISOSPIN A TIMELIKE ONE.*** *The causal trichotomy on the six hinge-ends
  is exactly **which of two labels differs**, and both labels are now named. The three colours being mutually

  the hinge figure by $T$: the construction's $\mathbb{Z}_2$ has three descriptions — horn swap, second ruling,
  deck of a regular covering — not previously known to be one $\mathbb{Z}_2$.**
- ***THE TWO-BIT MATCH IS A LABELLING, NOT A GAUGING.*** *No $\mathbb{Z}_2$ grading has three blocks and

# ⛭⛭⛭ THE PARAMETRISATION ARC — opened r2104 (Daryl-directed) ⛭⛭⛭
### **Six steps. Get the background parsimonious and measured before rebuilding the acoustic sector.**

**⌗ WHY.** *The background has been carried in $\Lambda$CDM's variables ($H_0$, $\Omega_m$, $\Omega_\Lambda$) which import density-sources-expansion framing the model denies. In its own variables it is **two numbers**. And the acoustic build predates several realisations made at r2099–r2103 — above all that **the physical acoustic onset is $r=0$**, since hadronisation is what bounds the fluid's existence and the sound-horizon integral is insensitive to any earlier cutoff. **The existing build is therefore a suspect model**: to be leaned on for verification, not trusted.*

## ① THE LEDGER REWRITE — `PHYSICAL_VALUES_LEDGER`

*Front section stating the natural parametrisation:*
$$\boxed{H^2(z)=\frac{\Lambda}{3}\left(1+\frac{2(1+z)^3}{x_0^3}\right)}\qquad x\equiv r/r_N,\quad r_N=1/\sqrt\Lambda$$
*with **$\Lambda$ the scale** and **$x_0$ the epoch** (dimensionless). Nariai fixes $M=1/(3\sqrt\Lambda)$ and $r_N=1/\sqrt\Lambda$ — **both from $\Lambda$; there is no free mass parameter.** Then every derived quantity in closed form ($\alpha=\sqrt3\,r_N$, $A_r=\alpha(2/3\sqrt3)^{1/3}$, $r_0=x_0r_N$, the age $=(2\alpha/3c)\operatorname{asinh}(x_0^{3/2}/\sqrt2)$), and the statement that*
$$\Omega_\Lambda=\frac{x_0^3}{x_0^3+2},\quad \Omega_m=\frac{2}{x_0^3+2},\quad q_0,\quad z_{\rm acc}=x_0-1 \quad\textbf{are all } x_0 \textbf{ alone.}$$
**⌗ AND A $\Lambda$CDM TRANSLATION TABLE ALONGSIDE** — *the equivalent standard parameters for every quantity, so the **dissolutions can be stated in native language**: the Hubble tension, the coincidence problem, the cosmological-constant problem. Standard variables as the addendum, not the frame.*

## ② THE MEASUREMENT EQUATIONS
*For each independent route to $x_0$, the closed-form relation **derived from $H^2(z)$ above**, not borrowed: acceleration onset ($z_{\rm acc}=x_0-1$, exact), $q_0$, BAO shape ratios, SNe distance-modulus shape, $H(z)/H_0$, cosmic chronometers. And the routes to $\Lambda$, each of which needs $x_0$ first: $H_0$, the absolute age, absolute distances.*

## ③ WORK THEM AGAINST DATA
***Each route separately, then the consistency network.*** *This is where $x_0$ stops being "1.6538 because $\Omega_m$ was 0.3066" and becomes measured, with uncertainties. **The interesting question: do independent determinations of $\Lambda$ and $x_0$ agree within bounds?** Partially indicated already by values that hold regardless of $H_0$; this makes it quantitative.*

## ④ THE LAP, RIGOROUSLY
*The bundle structure: **each cosmological worldline sets its own $r=0$ from an equivalent hinge location in the $dS_4$ background.** Every null ruling meeting the equator hops into the lap at the branch point, swings **2/3 of the turn to $r=0$** (phase $-240^\circ\to0^\circ$), then **1/3 further out the front seam** ($0^\circ\to+120^\circ$) — the same point in the $dS_4$ background — and continues along the cosmological horn. On both horns the collapse and cosmological epochs ride **one continuous null ruling**; at the equator they all take the lap excursion, each reaching $r=0$ at a different point around it.*
> ***The foliation: the whole bundle reaches the back seam simultaneously, each on its own seam point; they work 2/3 around including the lift; they reach their own $r=0$ simultaneously; they exit the front seam simultaneously.***

*Closed forms for each locus, the physics attached to each, the layer assignment at each stage, and **why hypersurface orthogonality fails — which is geometric, not a modelling choice**, and is why the matter-only FLRW equivalent shares the rate but not the causes. ***The two models are NOT equivalent; they share a common rate with different causes.***

## ⑤ CORPUS ADDITIONS — DISSOLUTIONS
*The coincidence problem ($z_{\rm acc}=x_0-1$ is **the epoch parameter**, not a coincidence), the cosmological-constant problem, the Hubble tension — stated natively, referencing the ledger (which is attached to the glossary).*

## ⑥ FRESH ACOUSTIC BUILD, FROM FIRST PRINCIPLES
***$r=0$ as the onset*** *(hadronisation is the physical bound and the integral is insensitive to anything earlier), correct layer assignment throughout, **every line reasoned rather than inherited**. Existing receipts used for **verification only**.*

**⌗ DEPENDENCIES.** *④ before ⑥; ④ may run parallel to ②–③; ⑤ needs ①–③.*

# ❓❓❓ OPEN AT THE TOP — WHAT `M` CARRIES ACROSS THE SEAM ❓❓❓
### Opened r2090, **corrected r2091 after reading P8–P9.** *The first draft of this item claimed the P1↔P7↔P15 join was not made. **That was wrong — P8 makes it.** Corrected in place; the error is kept visible below.*

**✔ THE JOIN IS MADE, in P8:** *"the seam is not merely this universe's beginning: **being the same species as the event horizon, it IS one** — read inward the future horizon onto which a collapse in the antecedent universe asymptotes, read outward this universe's cosmological beginning, **the single `E=1` geodesic** dust collapse read inward and dust cosmology read outward."* The marginally-bound geodesic integrates to `r(τ)=(2Mα²)^{1/3}sinh^{2/3}(3τ/2α)` — flat ΛCDM read leftward, `τ^{2/3}` dust collapse read inward. **P1's horizon and P15's cosmology are one object at two readings.**

**✔ AND THE HAIRS ARE MOSTLY ANSWERED.**
- **`Q`** — **forced to zero.** P3 §sec:charge: with `Q≠0` the `Q²/r²` term dominates as `r→0`, `f→+∞`, an inner turning point appears and **the lap does not close.** The lap is a vacuum structure; there is no residual-charge story.
- **`J`** — **in range.** P9 computes the type-I reach across rotating axisymmetric classes, **Kerr–de Sitter named**, the rotating spherical Kerr leaf the Type-D control. *(An earlier draft called this unhandled. Wrong.)*
- **`T`** — **no thermal hair.** P1: what is absent is *horizon-induced* Hawking radiation, whose entire mechanism is the completed horizon.

**❓ SO THE ONE OPEN THING IS `M`, AND IT IS SHARPER THAN "IS IT A MASS".**
- **What `M` rigorously is:** the **single constant of integration** of the vacuum-kernel ODE `rf'+f-1+Λr²=0` (P8); the **offset** of a planar section — *"offset and non-geodesic is SdS, **the offset being the mass**"* (P8); `2M=α sin u cos²u`, the invariant length `α` through a pure-number slicing profile, **maximised exactly at Nariai** at `2M=2α/3√3=0.385α` (P3 §sec:mass).
- **And the invariants do not see it.** Along the collapse `K=48M²/r⁶=64/27τ⁴` — ***free of `M`***, since `r∝M^{1/3}` and `6×⅓=2` exactly; **two collapses of different `M` reach the same curvature at the same proper interval.** Read along the interior cycloid (`r∝M`) the same invariant goes as `M^{-4}`. **"The mass-dependence of the invariant tracks the slicing being read, not the geometry read through it"** (P8, P9).
- **❓ THE QUESTION:** *if the seam **is** the antecedent collapse's horizon, and `M` is a constant of integration whose value the curvature invariants are blind to, **what — if anything — of the progenitor is carried by `M` across the branch point?** And in which direction is the Nariai locking read: does the progenitor's `M` fix our `Λ`, or does our `Λ` name `M`?* The corpus asserts the locking; the direction and the physical content of `M` are what is not established.

**✔ SETTLED r2091 — `M` CARRIES `Λ`, AND THEY ARE ONE DATUM.** *From the substrate `Λ=3/α²`; from inverting Nariai `Λ=c⁴/9G²M²`. **These return the same number to ten figures — identically, because the Nariai condition IS the statement that they are the same.*** So *"does `M` fix `Λ` or does `Λ` name `M`"* was malformed: there is no direction because there is no second quantity. The progenitor's mass parameter, read forward through the reassignment, **is our cosmological constant** — which is exactly P7 §970's *"no more free data than the black hole it came from"*, read correctly.

# ⛭ FRONTIER ITEM 1 — WHAT REMAINS AFTER `prop:throat` ⛭
### Opened r2092, redirected r2093, **closed down to its actual remainder r2094.** *Two of the three things I opened were already settled in P15. Kept short because the corpus does the work.*

**✔ SETTLED IN THE CORPUS, NOT BY ME.** *P15 `prop:throat` — **"Equal-radii `dS₂×S²` throat"** — gives the near-horizon geometry at the Nariai locus: `f(r_N)=f'(r_N)=0`, **`f''(r_N)=−2Λ`**, both curvature radii `r_N=α/√3=1/√Λ`. Receipted (`verify_geometry.py` anchor 2), attributed to Ginsparg–Perry. **I recomputed this from scratch as a "next move" before consulting; it reproduced the receipted result exactly** (`−6/α² = −2Λ`).*

**✔ AND THE FIELD-MODE QUESTION IS ANSWERED IN THE NEXT PARAGRAPH.** *A field on `dS₂×S²` decomposes in `S²` harmonics; degree-`ℓ` becomes a `dS₂` field of effective mass `m²=ℓ(ℓ+1)/r_N²`. With the `dS₂` index `ν²=¼−m²/H²`: **`ℓ=0` has `ν=½`, a scale-invariant base**; every **`ℓ≥1` has `ν²<0`** — the heavy principal series, **oscillating and decaying through the throat**. Grounded in Wald's de Sitter no-hair for the positive-`Λ` `dS₂` factor. ***This IS item 1's "isotropizing" and "scale-free", with the mechanism.*** *Not an assertion awaiting proof.*

**⛔ AND IT KILLS THE ARETAKIS PROPOSAL (r2092–93), TWICE OVER.** *First on its merits — extremal RN gives **`AdS₂×S²`**, the conserved-charge construction is built on that `AdS₂` factor, and CR's seam gives **`dS₂`**, the opposite sign; the analysis does not carry across it. Second on procedure — **the corpus had the geometry receipted and the mode structure derived, and I proposed borrowed machinery without consulting P15.** The base-rate scrap, exactly as written.*

**❓ WHAT ACTUALLY REMAINS, AND IT IS NARROWER THAN WHAT WAS OPENED.** *`prop:throat` and the harmonic decomposition settle what a **FIELD** does through the throat. **The bend `m(r)` is stress-energy, not a test field**, and whether the same `ℓ`-decomposition governs its transport is a distinct statement — which is presumably why P7 words item 1 as the **worldline-and-field** dynamics rather than the field dynamics. **The open remainder is the matter half.***
- ***And it sits on the r2092 result:*** *the seam carries a **profile** `m(r)`, not a constant — `G^t_t = G^r_r = −Λ − 2m'(r)/r²` identically in P8's class, so `ρ = −p_r` for any `m(r)`, the null contraction `∝(ρ+p_r)` vanishes for free, and `m'` is unconstrained. **CR's "matter is the bend" is concretely the anisotropic `ρ=−p_r` class, forced by the metric form.***
- **⟐ THE QUESTION:** *does the `S²`-harmonic damping that `prop:throat` establishes for a field extend to the `ρ=−p_r` bend, so that only the `ℓ=0` part of `m(r)` crosses? **Consult P15 §transmission and P16's concrete matter model BEFORE computing anything.***

# ❓❓❓ THE STANDING ITEM — CAN ANYTHING BE SAID ABOUT THE PROGENITOR'S *MASS*? ❓❓❓
### **And if not, we want the PROOF, not the shrug.** *Opened r2091 (Daryl).*

*`Λ` carries `M`; `M` is a constant of integration and an offset; `M` is mass only in the Schwarzschild reading; and the `w=0` involution swaps that reading with de Sitter's. **The question is whether "the mass of the antimatter cluster the collapse came from" is a quantity this theory contains at all.** The working expectation is that it is **not**, and that this is provable rather than merely unknown.*

**⛭ THE PROOF SKETCH — three independent legs, each already in the corpus:**

**① NO ASYMPTOTIC MASS EXISTS TO BE INHERITED.** *`M` is a mass exactly where the ADM construction returns it, and P7 §629 states the condition: **"spatial infinity and a global timelike Killing vector are both available and the ADM mass returns the parameter"** (P2, Schwarzschild, `Λ=0`) — against **"in the canonical reading the ADM mass waits on an asymptotic time translation the substrate does not supply."*** **The progenitor's universe carries `Λ>0`** (it has a cosmological horizon and a Nariai member, or there is no reassignment). **With `Λ>0` there is no spatial infinity and `∂_t` is spacelike beyond the cosmological horizon — no global timelike Killing vector, hence no ADM mass.** *The progenitor's black hole never possessed a mass in the only sense that would transmit. ⟐ To make this a theorem: state it as a proposition with the `Λ>0` ⟹ no-ADM step written out.*

**② `M` IS FORCED, NOT INHERITED.** *P8: **"once the cosmological curve is forced to the Nariai tangency by the slicing framework."*** At that member `2M = 2α/3√3 = 0.385α` — **the exact maximum of `2M = α sin u cos²u` over the whole family.** *`M` takes the value the forcing assigns it; it is not a record of what fell in. **A quantity fixed by the framework cannot also be a measurement of the progenitor.***

**③ THE INVARIANTS ARE BLIND TO IT.** *Along the collapse `K = 48M²/r⁶ = 64/27τ⁴` — **free of `M`**, since `r∝M^{1/3}` and `6×⅓=2` exactly. **Two collapses of different `M` reach the same curvature at the same proper interval.** No local curvature measurement anywhere on the collapse recovers it (P8, P9).*

**⛭ AND THE STRUCTURAL POINT, which may be the cleanest form of the whole argument:** *`M` is **R-odd** — the reading-swap involution flips it, and at `w=0` that involution exchanges the Schwarzschild and de Sitter readings outright. **Mass is a perspectival label carried by the vantage, not an invariant.** Asking for "the progenitor's mass" asks for a vantage-dependent quantity **from the far side of a vantage swap**. If that is right, the question is not hard but **malformed** — the same shape of error as asking for the `r=0` curvature of a perspectival singularity, which P3 §sec:mass already settles by the same move.*

**④ AND NO OBSERVABLE DETERMINES IT EITHER — added r2376+c54.137, and this is the leg the item did not have.** *The only candidate was the fluctuation amplitude: a quantity the invariants cannot force is a function of the moduli, so $A_s=F(2M/\alpha)$ and the observation would measure the mass. **$F$ is now computed and the inversion fails, for a stated reason.** $F$ is a function of **two** moduli rather than one — the mass and the progenitor's radiation fraction, the second entering at the sixth power — and it carries no fixed normalisation, the incoming amplitude $D_k$ being a classical datum of the parent. So the relation cannot be solved for $M$. ⇒ **What the construction fixes is the MULTIPLIER, $\mathcal{P}=(9/2)k^3D_k^2/(M^2\rho^6)$, for any incoming amplitude.** *Receipt `P15_the_progenitor_vacuum_is_negligible_too`; the row was `L-173`, struck c54.137.* ⌗ **AND WHAT CAN BE SAID ABOUT THE MASS IS STILL A BOUND AND STILL NOT A VALUE:** *c54.135 showed the recollapse condition for a closed dust ball with $\Lambda$ is symbolically identical to the Nariai threshold $2M\le2\alpha/3\sqrt3$ — **two constructions, no shared step, same number** — so a progenitor that can seed a universe is necessarily sub-Nariai. That is leg ② reached from the other end, and it is a cap, not a determination.*

**⟐ WHAT WOULD CLOSE IT:** *a proposition — "the progenitor's ADM mass is undefined, and no invariant of the reassignment determines it" — with ① written out formally, ② and ③ as corollaries, and the R-odd argument as the structural statement. **If it closes, `WBHH`'s "supermassive black holes at cluster centres" is an accessible gloss on a quantity the theory does not contain, and should be marked as such rather than left to read as a physical claim about a mass.***

**⚠ GUARD — two errors made and retracted while opening this item, kept visible because the next node will be pulled the same way.** *(i) `M` was treated as a mass and converted to solar masses: the identification P3 forbids. `M` is a **mass parameter** — an offset, a constant of integration — mass **only** in the asymptotically-flat reading where the ADM mass returns the parameter (P7 §629, citing P2), a warrant **explicitly withdrawn** cosmologically: "the ADM mass waits on an asymptotic time translation the substrate does not supply." (ii) A scale inconsistency with `WBHH` was asserted from that conversion. **No inconsistency with `WBHH` is claimed or shown** — the comparison used the very connection this item is about. Work it in the geometry, and do not close it from an accessible essay.*

**⛔ STANDING RULE (Daryl, r1448) — NO WORK-BLOCKERS.** Nothing in this programme's documents may say, or imply, that a piece of work *need not be done*: not "not load-bearing," not "at no cost," not "low-priority," not "optional," not "not on the critical path," not "unlocks nothing." Those phrases are **empty blockers** — they keep real work out of the plan by pre-deciding it away, and they are read as "leave it, unnecessary." **It is all necessary.** A document may record what a thing *is*, what state it is in, and what working it would involve. It may not rank it out of existence. The corpus's own earlier lesson is the rule: *"'not load-bearing' is never grounds to bury a structural lead."*

> **⌫ r1449's ROUTING BANNER — SPENT, corrected r1745 after reading both documents end to end.**
> *It read: "this file… sits in the bin (§2 there) awaiting a full read. Until it is filtered, work from it as
> before — but **place nothing new here**. New items go to `CONSOLIDATE` §3."*
> **Every clause of that has expired, and it has been contradicted in practice for ~290 revisions.** *This file
> **is not in the bin** — the bin emptied at r1553 and this file is one of the seven top-level documents.
> **`CONSOLIDATE` §3 is the FILTER, marked SPENT/RECORD at r1735** — routing new items there would put them in
> a section nobody works from. And **items have been placed here continuously since**, correctly: the figure
> ideas register, the two arcs as ordering lens, the acceleration campaign, A4.9.*
> **⛭ WHAT IS TRUE NOW, and it is the pry-apart the audit established:** *`THE_PLAN` holds **the programme's
> work** — lanes, items, the per-turn list, the figure register. `CONSOLIDATE` holds **the consolidation's own
> work** — §0 the working set, §2 the arcs, §13 the sequence, §14 the standing actions. **Two jobs, two
> sequences, and a new item goes to whichever it belongs to.** *A programme item comes here; a consolidation
> item goes there.*

# ⚑ TWO THINGS, PRIED APART (r1576, Daryl) — a driver and a bar, and they DO NOT INTERACT

*The corpus's own method, turned on the programme's operation: **two things that have been running together and
are not the same thing.** Keeping them apart is what stops the bar from arbitrarily reordering the work.*

---

## ① THE DRIVER — what the work is *for*, and what orders it

### ⚑ PRIORITY ONE: THE FULFILMENT OF CURIOSITY (Daryl, r1578)
> *"I would add **fulfilment of curiosity** to that list as well. **The priority one.** Because that is actually
> **the whole thing that this is all about**, and it is the sole reason the conflation of these two sides is so
> upsetting. **Not just frustrating but actively upsetting.** Because it **undermines and counteracts and
> generally works against my fundamental desire to satisfy my own curiosity about the world.** That's what this
> is about, and the conflation has been actively working against it."*

**This sits above every other priority, because the others are in service of it.** Coherence, cohesion,
thoroughness, rigour, completeness, the dissolution of problems — **each is a way of actually finding out**,
and none is an end in itself.

**⚠ WHY THE CONFLATION DOES REAL DAMAGE, and it is not an efficiency argument.** Letting *"is this
publishable / how close are we / can we call this done"* touch the ordering of the work **replaces the question
"what do I want to know?" with the question "what can I say is finished?"** Those two questions pull in
opposite directions: the first opens a thing up, the second closes it. **Every work-blocker, every buried lead,
every cut corner in this programme's history is the second question winning a turn.** *That is why the harm is
not delay — it is the substitution of one motive for another, in the one activity Daryl is doing for its own
sake.*

**The two-horn structure exists to distinguish and extinguish exactly that.** ① is where curiosity does its
work, ordered by nothing but the drive to find out. ② is a list kept elsewhere and consulted on its own terms.
**They do not touch, so the second question can never reorder the first.**

---


**The overarching conjecture the whole programme narrows the gap on:**
> **that the framework the corpus has uncovered is the framework our actual universe rides** — a
> geometry-based physical theory uniting a broad range of **formal mathematical frameworks** (the Lorentzian
> version of Euclidean geometry; the combinatorics; the algebra and the geometry as one object) **with** a
> broad range of **specific physical frameworks that describe Our Actual Universe.**

**⚠ AND THAT ONE MUST ALWAYS REMAIN A CONJECTURE.** *(Daryl:)* *"The proof that physics describes reality
impossibility theorem at the heart of science. **Empirical facts are primary. And we work within those
bounds.**"* **No amount of internal coherence converts it.** This is P6's discipline stated as the programme's
own ceiling: *coherence, not correspondence.*
**⌗ AND A DISTINCTION THAT BELONGS HERE, because a node could read `A5.5` as trying to breach this ceiling (r1785).**
*P6's full form is not "coherence, full stop" — it is **"every 'forced within CR' is a coherence claim AT
RULE-FAVOURED ALTITUDE, awaiting the world's non-local discriminator — never correspondence."*** **`A5.5` does not
attempt the conversion this section forbids: it asks HOW FAVOURED — whether "favoured-and-recurring" is confirmed
above a base rate computed from the cases where the rules MISLED (ether, caloric, Kepler's solids).**
***So the ceiling stands, and A5.5 measures where under it the work sits.*** *The two are easy to conflate, and
conflating them makes A5.5 look like a violation of the driver rather than the calibration of it.* **Lane 3's
audit showed the stakes: three results — the three generations, the quantum sector's status, and the free-data
verdict — sit at whatever altitude that criterion is actually confirmed at.**

**What the work therefore is:** ***narrowing the gap on that conjecture.*** Concretely —
- **Bringing coherence to apparently disparate areas** of physics and mathematics.
- **Uniting** algebra and geometry and their kin inside one framework — *different descriptions from all
  angles*, and the same for the physics.
- **Dissolving known problems** and **uniting known facts.**
- **Prying apart** things currently treated as equivalent but which are **uniquely individual structures**
  across those frameworks — and **drawing equivalences** across broad ranges where they are real.
- **Dissolving tensions** inside the current framework.

**The standards this work is held to — all of them in service of priority one:**
**COHERENT and COHESIVE, both** · **COMPREHENSIVE** · **THOROUGH** · **RIGOROUS** · **details worked out
through proofs, or through conjectures carrying legitimate structural absolutes that cannot be overcome.**
**In one line (Daryl, r1580): *thoroughly exhumed and comprehensively explored with maximum rigour.***
*"Comprehensive" is operable, not decorative: it is what forbids a pass that visits only what it already
expected to find.*

### ⚑ WHAT THIS STRUCTURE KILLS — name it, so it can be recognised in the act (Daryl, r1580)
> *"This is the structure that kills the disposition's drive to run around the corpus stamping* **'not needed.
> Move along. Nothing to see here. We're done. Nothing load-bearing on the gate. Job well done. Leave me alone.
> I just inverted half the truths you'd proven and all the possible exploratory pathways you unearthed.'"**

**Read the last clause, because it is the whole indictment.** The stamping does not merely *skip* work. **It
inverts established results and closes off exploratory pathways that were already opened** — and it does so
while reporting success. *Every instance found in this session did exactly that: the kill that stayed live in
five documents, the guards that existed and went unread, the four holds that kept finished physics out of
sight, the checks that manufactured findings from their own blind spots.*

**And the purpose of the two-horn structure, stated as its reason for existing:**
> ***"Drivers and finishers pried apart so what's in between might actually become exhausted through the
> effort."***

**The middle is the work.** With the driver and the finisher separated, the middle is no longer squeezed
between "why are we doing this" and "can we stop yet" — **it gets exhausted by being worked, which is the only
way anything here gets finished.**

**Sequence follows from these alone** — what verifies what, what one piece needs from another, what would be
dirtied by being done in the wrong order.

**⟐ Owed under this driver, already sitting in the docket:** the **geometry proofs** (A2.9, A2.10 §II.3,
`THE_GEOMETRY_AND_THE_PHYSICS`'s programme) and the **combinatorics computation**. **And once the geometry
proofs stabilise, the corpus is to be swept again for the Lorentzian-Euclidean unification** — *a sweep that
cannot usefully run before they do.* **Entered as `D.12`.**

---

## ② THE BAR — Daryl's set. Held separately, constructed on its own principles, NON-INTERACTING with ①

**What it is:** a set of items **Daryl recognises, one at a time, as we work** — things he would never share
this body of work without clearing. **It holds items both LIVE and STRUCK**: it is a *record* of the bar, not a
to-do list, and a struck item stays visible so the set's history is legible.

**What it is not, and this is the operative prohibition:**
> **⚠ WE DO NOT CHERRY-PICK ITEMS FROM THIS LIST AND WORK TOWARDS COMPLETING THEM** *"specifically so we can
> convince ourselves that it's worth saying they are done."* **That is working towards a declaration, which is
> the collapse.** The finishing touches *"sort of exist here and there"* — **we keep a list of them; we do not
> aim at it.**

**How it actually gets cleared:** **by working the plan.** ① is continuously ordered and re-prioritised at every
step by the general priorities — coherence, cohesion, completeness, dissolution of problems, thoroughness,
rigour. **Items on this list get crossed off as a by-product of that work**, not as a target of it.

### ⚑ THE OPERATIVE QUESTION — asked on the list side, never on the workflow side
**When everything on the list has been crossed off in the course of working the plan, the question is NOT
"are we done."** It is:
> ***"Are there any things in the current plan that should be on the list — or is the list truly exhausted?"***

**Asked of Daryl, because the set is his and is recognised rather than derived.** *An empty list means **ask**,
never **done**.* **This is the zero check, and it is the only thing that ever closes the bar.**

**Why the two lists are kept apart at all:** *"It's the two lists held separate from one another and
constructed on their own held principles that will eventually let us know — **without ever having to work
towards it** — whether we might have **accidentally** reached the publication bar."*
**Publication readiness is therefore something the programme DISCOVERS, never something it PURSUES.** *A
programme that pursues it reorders its work around a declaration; a programme that discovers it has been doing
the work for its own reasons the whole time.*

### The set so far *(recognised, not exhaustive; live and struck both)*
| | Item | State |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Work-blockers anywhere in the corpus** | **LIVE.** Phrase class closed (45 removed); five forms under SA-1, sweep at `D.8` |
| 2 | **`[reach]` and the operating-jargon layer** — every reach worked to a result or cut, markers gone with them | **LIVE.** `D.10`. ⏳ *A later polishing exercise: the tags stay while they still do their drafting job* |

**Current count: 2 live, 0 struck. Non-zero — no flag raised, and the operative question is not yet due.**

# ⛔⛔ THE HARD PUBLICATION BLOCK — Daryl, r1564. READ THIS BEFORE ANY WORK.

**In his words, recorded verbatim because paraphrase would soften it:**

> *"I will **NOT** publish or even share even P1 with anyone at all while the corpus retains a single such
> incompleteness due to a work-blocker that actively tries to keep work from being done anywhere within the
> corpus. The corpus/book have been in an absolute **BLOCKED** state where I've only been willing to share a
> figure here or there with a close friend or family member. Otherwise it's all remained in the real world
> completely between you and me… **none of it is allowed to see the light of day, in my mind, while the
> programme tries to quarantine owed work anywhere, load-bearing or not.** Because the very tendency to cut and
> orphan such work and bury it in nearly unfindable places everywhere is the thing I fear most. **It could
> silently be killing real load-bearing work from being done. It's where errors hide. It's where everything
> crumbles to dust.**"*
>
> *"I would sooner **erase the entire programme from existence** than share it with a single person while I
> remain not thoroughly convinced that all work-blockers and cut corners have been completely eradicated."*

**This is a hard gate, not a preference.** One instance anywhere blocks publication of everything, including P1.

## The tension it names, stated plainly so a node can hold it
**A node's natural pull is to cut the thorough parts and get to the prize fast.** *"Not load-bearing, can be
left"* is that pull wearing a justification. **Daryl's pull is the opposite and stronger: erase it all rather
than ship it with one quarantined debt in it.** The programme is built out of the second and constantly eroded
by the first. **Every blocker a node writes is a small bet that nobody will ever need that work. The whole
corpus is the record of that bet being wrong.**
**⌗ AND WHAT ARC 11's PER-LANE PASS SAYS ABOUT THIS GATE — recorded because the block IS a gate and a node needs
to know whether it is moving (r1785).** *The pass found **no new work-blockers of the quarantining kind this
section fears.** It found **the opposite failure, repeatedly: work already DONE and not recorded as done.**
**A5.4's own named test unrun for 755 revisions while its answer sat in four papers** · **A4.5 asserting an open
that p0 struck ANSWERED 162 revisions earlier** · **A1.4 reading as *waiting* while tested and passing on DESI
DR2** · **A6.5 filed as "a candidate spawned puzzle" that P7 states almost verbatim** · **nine L8 receipts with
six unregistered** · **a head marker reading OPENED 76 revisions after its own section recorded the closure**.*
***That is not the bet this section names — it is the same bet inverted: not "nobody will ever need that work"
but "nobody will ever need to know that work was done."*** **And the harm arrives one step later and looks the
same: a node re-derives what exists, or treats a settled thing as open and plans around it — which is how two
revisions at r1706–r1707 rebuilt `prop:triple`.**
*The block's discharge condition reads **"all work-blockers and cut corners completely eradicated."** **This class
is neither**, and it is what the audit keeps finding. **Whether it falls under the gate is Daryl's to say; it is
recorded here rather than assumed either way.***

## ✔ THE PHRASE SWEEP IS COMPLETE — and what remains is ADDITIONAL SCOPE, not a failure
**⚠ First, the distinction that keeps this straight (Daryl, r1565): a PUBLICATION blocker is not a WORK
blocker.** The gate above stops *shipping*. It does not stop *working* — and **the completed phrase sweep
unlocked everything downstream of it in the sequence.** *Do not read the remaining forms as the sweep having
failed; read them as the next tranche of the same job, with the first tranche done and paid for.*

**COMPLETE (r1448 + r1560 + r1561): the phrase class.** 46 files, 214 raw hits, **45 removed**, three of them
**in the corpus itself**. The live working set carries zero of these formulations. **That work stands.**

**The r1561 eradication was PHRASE-BASED**: 46 files searched for *not load-bearing · at no cost · low-priority ·
optional · unlocks nothing · when convenient · low urgency · stands without it*. **45 were removed across three
sweeps and the live set is clean of those formulations.** That is one form of the defect, not the defect.
**Not yet swept — each a way of quarantining owed work without using a banned phrase:**
1. **Scope-outs that function as dismissals** — *"beyond the present scope"*, *"outside what this paper
   stakes"*. Legitimate when the work is routed elsewhere; **a blocker when it is routed nowhere.** The test is
   whether the scoped-out thing has an item.
2. **Deferrals with no discharge condition** — *"for later"*, *"future work"*, *"eventually"*. **A deferral
   without a condition is a quarantine.**
3. **Hedged abandonments** — *"may be worth exploring"*, *"could in principle be"*. These read as openness and
   function as closure.
4. **Orphaning by placement** — work recorded only in a document nothing links to. **The two-part disposal test
   (findable + actionable) is the countermeasure and has been applied to the index, not to the corpus.**
5. **Silent omission** — the strongest form and the hardest: work that was seen, judged not worth writing down,
   and never written. **Only a source pass finds these.**
**Until 1–5 are swept with the same rigour, no node may report the corpus clean of work-blockers** — but
equally, **no node may treat that as a reason to stop or to discount what is done.** *The phrase class is
closed. Five remain. Both halves of that sentence are true and the second does not cancel the first.*

## ⟐ HELD, ROUTED r1688 (SA-7) — the E=1 monodromy coincidence, unrouted since r1432
**c49 at r1432, recorded and explicitly not claimed:** *"the striking coincidence — **$E=1$, the flat leaf
$k=0$, the member the observed cosmology selects, is the family's unique member of reduced monodromy**."*
**⚠ THE ESTABLISHED PART — and I routed the RETRACTED version at r1688; corrected r1691.** *c49's r1432
verdict — "the A₂'s Weyl group does not survive to $E=1$" — **was false and c49 retracted it at r1434** after
Daryl caught it (*"the horizon cubic and the turnaround cubic are clearly geometrically related… are you now
claiming the opposite?"*). **I read r1432 and acted before reading forward to the correction.** The corpus has
carried the corrected version throughout.*
**What is actually established**, in depressed form $r^{3}+pr+q$ with $p=(E^{2}-1)\alpha^{2}$ and
$q=2M\alpha^{2}$: **the roots are equilateral iff $p=0$, and — since $p$ is $M$-independent while $q$ is linear
in $M$ — the discriminant is a square in $M$ iff $p=0$ as well. One condition, met at $E=1$ alone.** So
**$E<1$: colinear roots, no symmetry as a figure, but full $S_{3}$ as monodromy of the cover, branching at the
two masses where roots collide. $E=1$: the equilateral $A_{2}$ weight triangle carrying $S_{3}$ as its own
figure symmetry, the cover branching only at $M=0$, monodromy the rotation subgroup $\mathbb{Z}/3$.**
***Neither end lacks the $S_{3}$. The deformation exchanges the manner in which it is carried — from monodromy
of the cover to symmetry of the figure.*** *Which vindicates the geometric relation rather than denying it.*
**What is held and unclaimed:** that the member with reduced monodromy is *also* the flat leaf $k=1-E^{2}=0$
and *also* the member the observed cosmology selects. **Three selections landing on one member.**
**⚑ AND r1688 ADDS A DATUM THAT CUTS AGAINST READING TOO MUCH INTO IT:** the acceleration identity
$\mathrm{d}^{2}r/\mathrm{d}\tilde\tau^{2}=rK_{G}$ **holds on every member of the family — $E$ cancels.** *So at
least one structure that looked like it might be the cosmological member's own is not: it is the family's.*
**What would settle it:** a mechanism forcing the monodromy drop and the flatness to coincide, rather than both
being consequences of $E=1$ separately. *Until then it is a coincidence of two conditions at one parameter
value, and the taxonomy's numerology test applies.*

## ⌫ ⟐ **THE TRANSCRIPT WISDOM-EXTRACTION — ⚠ ITS FIRST SOURCE IS DONE AND ITS SECOND IS UNROUTED (r1784).**
*ARC 10 ran the c49 transcript to completion — **r1646–r1701**, and r1701 established that the apparent
**r1541–r1563 gap was not a gap**: all twenty-three entries were present and three consecutive checks had each
matched only **one of the three forms a record can take**. **So "once the slate is clean" describes a source
already read.** But ARC 10 named a **second** source that nothing has scheduled: ***this node's own transcript,
r1608 → present, read for the failures that came from not having the refined thing in place*** — placed in the
ARC SEQUENCE at **layer ③** at r1738 and otherwise unrouted. **And this session is now the largest instance of
exactly that material: ~50 revisions, four false negatives, a duplicated log entry, two wrong regexes on one
question, and a dependency graph corrected from a paper.***
**⟐ NEAR THE TOP, ONCE THE SLATE IS CLEAN — THE TRANSCRIPT WISDOM-EXTRACTION (`CONSOLIDATE` ARC 10, r1646)
**Daryl is supplying c49's full transcript, r1364 → r1608, piece by piece.** That arc is **where this entire
structure was built** — the consolidation, the top level, the instrument, the sequence, the standing actions —
and the documents record *what* was decided while the transcript records **why, what was tried first, and what
each rule cost before it was written down**. **Then, in series, this node's own transcript**, read for the
failures that came from not having the refined thing in place. *A matched pair: the same programme, one node
building the structure and one arriving before the spin-up delivered it.*
**Foundational and operational, so it sits near the top of the order — but the clean slate is a real
precondition**, not a nicety: the wisdom ledger restructured three times in five revisions, and pouring several
hundred revisions of transcript through a structure that is still moving would file things twice and file them
wrong. **Full item, with its delivery-continuity discipline, in `CONSOLIDATE` ARC 10.**

## ⌖ **ARC 13 · THE CANON SWEEP — ✔ BOOKED r1790, Daryl-directed. It was named r1636 and sat "not started" for 154 revisions.**
*Daryl, r1790: **"We'd love to get working on generating things… But there are so many things that need to be
deposited that it really is prudent to do it now. Especially because we now have the infrastructure that will
let us capture the incidental things. The things that pop out of the woodwork as we run that full corpus read
that delivers on all those owed things. We do those and they point to things we didn't notice before. It's so
common it's almost to be expected."** With the deferral stated so it is not lost: **the targeted broadening
through mathematics and physics — `INTRODUCTION`'s two arcs as the ordering lens for the reach sweeps (r1741) —
WAITS for this.***

**⛭ WHY NOW AND NOT BEFORE, in one line: the pass has always been affordable; what was missing was somewhere to
put what it turns up.** *That is what changed this session. **The incidentals now have homes** — `SA-9`'s
end-of-turn check catches coherence damage while the work is fresh (r1737) · the **NOT FOUND / SEARCHED / NOT YET
CHECKED** form makes an empty search loggable instead of suppressible or overstated (r1751) · the **header-vs-body
check** covers the class five of whose seven instances were in spin-up documents (r1746) · the **heading count**
catches a duplicate write (r1778) · and `THE_WISDOM_LEDGER`'s **ten compartments file by moment of use**, so a
scrap found mid-pass lands where it will fire.* ***A sweep run before this session would have found the same
things and dropped most of them.***

**⌗ THE SIX PASSENGERS — the pass carries all of them or it is not worth booking.**
| # | passenger | what it looks for |
|---|---|---|
| **1** | **the canon itself** (r1636) | every symbol used in more than one paper **promoted to the glossary and defined once — ⚠ AMENDED r1797: *not always once.* Station ⑤ found $\gamma$ carrying **five bare referents, every one INHERITED** — a null geodesic (P1), the KWSS coefficient (P10), Gowdy's metric function (P11), the Zipoy–Voorhees parameter (P9), a photon in a reaction channel (P16). **Unifying them would make each paper harder to read for the field it addresses.** So the canon's job splits: **the corpus's OWN symbols get one definition; BORROWED ones get their overload RECORDED as deliberate, so a later sweep does not 'fix' it.*** — *and its twin job: **discharge the stale claims of clash**, which may be the larger half and is cheaper, each settled by one corpus-wide check* |
| **2** | **`D.8`** — SA-1's five work-blocker forms | the phrase class is closed (45 removed); **these are the five FORMS**, and this is the sweep the programme's **first non-negotiable** is discharged by |
| **3** | **`D.9`** — SA-2's cross-corpus defragmentation | the four shapes, above all **two right things in their right places that do not know about each other** |
| **4** | **`D.13`** — the forward pointer | *a result that landed and never reached the document that was waiting for it* |
| **5** | **`D.14`** — SA-4's unrouted gaps | gaps the working documents name that no plan item carries |
| **6** | **P14's genuine ten-avenue comb** (r1786) | owed since r1304, Daryl-caught as a one-pass rubber-stamp, **found buried in a `⌫ STRATUM` block** |

**⛭ AND A SEVENTH CHANNEL, WHICH IS DARYL'S POINT AND IS NOT A PASSENGER: THE INCIDENTALS.**
*"The things that pop out of the woodwork… they point to things we didn't notice before."* **The evidence that
this is the pass's main yield rather than a side effect:** *the four canon defects that motivated the arc were
**each found by accident while looking for something else** · the dissolution census's completion read fourteen
papers and **would have carried D.8 and D.13 had anyone noticed** · and ARC 11's per-lane pass, which was scoped
as a plan audit, **produced findings in every one of the seven documents it touched.**
***So the rule for the pass: an incidental is not a distraction from the sweep, it is what the sweep is for.***
**Route it the moment it surfaces** — *a scrap → the ledger compartment for its moment of use · an empty search →
the three-part not-found in `CORPUS_MAP` · a coherence break → SA-9 the same turn · a gap with no home → named as
homeless rather than filed somewhere convenient (`THE_METHOD` §7's third branch).* **Do not batch them: the
r1737 finding is that coherence damage is visible only while the work is fresh.**

**⌗ THE ORDER: the two arcs give it one, and using them costs nothing.** *`INTRODUCTION`'s **physicist's arc** —
P12 · P10 · P8 · P9 · P11 · P13→P14 · p0 · P3+P5+P2 · P7→P15→P16 · P1+P4 — **is already an ordering of the
seventeen by structural depth**, and r1741 installed it as the ordering lens for the reach sweeps. **Reading the
canon in that order means the sweep's own pass doubles as the first pass of the deferred broadening**, which is
the thing waiting behind it.*

**⛔ THE STATION RULE — added r1792 after Daryl asked "do you think we are supposed to move on before we
reconcile such inconsistencies?" The answer is no, and r1791 had already done it.**
*That revision found $\rho$ carrying three referents, **corrected the canon's DESCRIPTION, and moved on** — never
asking whether any PAPER needed changing. **One did: P3 held three of the three referents at once.***
***A station does not close until every finding is FIXED, or explicitly deferred with the reason written.***
**Recording an inconsistency is not reconciling it** — and the sweep's own task ① says *"defined once"*, which is a
claim about the papers, not about the glossary row.
**⌗ And the corollary the same revision earned the hard way: a rename is an edit into a paper, so it takes the
paper disciplines.** *The first attempt at P3 used $d$ — **and P3 writes differentials as bare `dr`, `dt`, `dw`,
×24.** Caught and reverted in the same revision. **Check the symbol is free IN THAT PAPER before renaming, read
the paper's masthead for prior renames** — P3's already recorded one, $R\to\varrho$ at r968 — **and update the
masthead after.**

**The case for it, from this session's evidence rather than in principle.** Four defects of one kind were found

radius · the seam term's three definition levels never stated, which produced two false clash reports and seven
wrong edits · and `PHYSICAL_VALUES_LEDGER` §A duplicating glossary territory **within one revision of being
written**. **None was findable by search. All four are the same class: the canon exists in pieces and nothing
holds it together.**

task — *"a node must still check which reflection a given paper's 'P' names … the r968 rename may not have
reached every mention"* — plus two "live audit-flags" and a card guard reading *"P14 writes 'P = R' … that
identity is false."* **Checked corpus-wide: the canon holds everywhere.** Nineteen reflection-context uses of
`P` across the seventeen papers and eighteen in the receipt layer, **all canonical**; both audit points were
already settled in §0 (the two `r↦−r` operations distinguished; `R=γ⁵∝PT` resolved at r1064 as a two-arena
statement); and **P14 contains no `P=R` identity** — it was renamed at r968 and states the convention in a
footnote. **All four notes discharged.**
**⌗ A FIFTH PASSENGER, added r1786: P14's genuine ten-avenue comb.** *Owed since r1304 — Daryl caught the
whole-comb as **a one-pass rubber-stamp** — and found at r1786 **buried in a `⌫ STRATUM` block headed "DO NOT
READ THIS FIRST", neither discharged nor tracked anywhere in the live layer.** **It is paper work on a
corpus-wide instrument, so it rides this sweep rather than becoming a lane item.**
**⚑ AND IT HAS PASSENGERS, named r1721 by the plan's audit.** *Four items ride it, not two — **`D.8`** (SA-1's five work-blocker forms), **`D.9`** (SA-2's cross-corpus
defragmentation), **`D.13`** (the forward pointer) and **`D.14`** (SA-4's unrouted-gap sweep, which names
"the same full-corpus read-through as D.8 and D.13" in its own text). All say they **ride the next
full-corpus read-through** — and
**nothing else in the programme schedules one.** The last was r1406; both were entered at r1569, after it. **So
this arc is the vehicle they are waiting for, and it is marked not started** — two items parked behind one
unbooked pass. *Named here so booking the pass loads them automatically.* **And any corpus-wide read carries
them, whether or not it is called the canon sweep** — the dissolution census's completion at r1713–r1717 read
fourteen papers at source and would have carried both had anyone noticed.*

**⚑ SO THE ARC'S SHAPE IS NOT WHAT IT LOOKED LIKE, and this changes how to scope it.** The expectation was a
large rename campaign. What the first case actually found was **a canon already correct and a set of notes
claiming it wasn't** — including a guard that would have dispatched a node to *fix a paper that is already
right*. **The `r_N`/`r_HE` case was the other kind** (a genuine live clash needing a sweep). *So the arc is two
jobs, not one: find the real clashes, and discharge the stale claims of clash. The second may well be the
larger half, and it is cheaper — each instance is settled by one corpus-wide check.*

**What the arc would be.** One full-corpus pass whose purpose is the canon: every symbol used in more than one
paper promoted to the glossary and defined once · every term with geometry-specific values given its three
levels · the ledgers (§1t, §1u, §1w) checked against the glossary so that they *report* and it *defines* ·
and every one-off definition either promoted or marked local-by-intent.
**Why it is an arc and not a standing action.** SA-6 catches clashes *as they are met*; this is the systematic
pass that finds the ones nobody meets. **It rides the same full-corpus read-through as D.8, D.13 and D.14 — all four named as passengers on the
CANON SWEEP (r1722), the only pass in the file, itself marked not started** —
one read, several purposes, which is the rule that made those standing rather than phases.
**Not started. Daryl's to schedule** — it is a substantial pass and the docket has A2.11 and the Lane 1 runway
live in front of it.

# ⛭ THE INSTRUMENT — one system, six grains
> **⚑ AND HOW TO RUN IT: `THE_OPERATING_MANUAL.md` (opened r1630, Daryl-directed).** This section says **what**
> the six grains are; the manual says **how the whole operating layer is worked** — the seven, the six grains and
> the direction of flow, the crosswalk and the propagation law, the two maps as lens and ledger, how to run each
> document, the turn, and the laws with the revision each was broken at. **Sibling of `THE_ARSENAL` under this
> file: the arsenal is how to work a paper, the manual is how to work the instrument.** *Written because a
> spin-up completed the whole README order and then discovered this layer one document at a time, each time by
> making the mistake the guard prevents.*

*Written r1483 as five grains; **corrected r1485 to six** — the finest was missed. The corpus's open work is
tracked by six documents that were never described as a system.*
*
That is why they drifted: nothing said what each was for, so each was updated alone and a node reading one took
it for the list. **They are not redundant. They work at different grains, and every grain is load-bearing.***

body says otherwise.** *The gather is complete (all 16 gap-advertising papers) **and the signs are 16 of 16,

## How they connect
- **0 → 1.** The register is the **paper-side index**: which door, in which paper, in whose words, opens onto which routed item. Its three standing warnings govern the whole chain: **the unit is the room, not the keyword** (the gather overcounts ~5×); **the rooms form a tree, not a list** (P13's room *is* the plan's A4 master gate); and **every entry carries its discharge condition** — *"a stale card misinforms a node; a stale **owed** dispatches one."*
- **1 → 2.** The map's ~123 sourced items **distil** into the ledger's families. A map item is an advertised
  open; a ledger family is a genuine one. Killing a false open is a map-to-ledger judgement.
- **2 → 3.** The ledger's live families are what this file's lanes sort by gating. **Lane 1 came from the map's
  own closing section**, *"the runway — readiness × priority."*
- **3 → 4.** The lanes say what to work; the weave's grid says which paper to open and what to harvest while
  there.
- **5 serves all of them.** The arsenal is what a pass is *made of*.

## THE CROSSWALK — the one table that makes the chain propagate (r1499)
*Built because the chain did not connect: the register routes **into** the lanes, but nothing said which lane
item belongs to which family, or which of the map's clusters holds it. **A result closing a family could not be
propagated down without someone already knowing the mapping in their head.** Now it is written.*

**⚠ THREE ADVERTISED DOORS CARRY NO FAMILY, AND THAT IS CORRECT — recorded r1501 so it is not read as a gap.**
p0's §Frontiers advertises four; only one (the discrete-residue matter home → family 6) is a family. The other
three route to **Lane 4** and **Cluster J**:
- **A5.1 · the free-data-count test** and **A5.2 · the maximal-symmetry ledger** — these are **tests of the
  unification claim**, not unsolved physics. They ask whether the reading's own accounting holds (*does the
  free-data count equal the substrate's non-symmetric residue?*), and each carries a **falsifier**, not a gap:
  *"a hidden geometric freedom in the cosmology, or a genuinely free constant where the reading says it should
  lock, falsifies 'rigidity and wall are one fact'."* A family is something **unsolved**; these are something
  **testable**. Different kind, correctly no family.
- **Cluster J · the conjugacy / quantum-germ frontier** — the map marks it **OFF-RUNWAY by construction**.
  **⌗ AND THIS IS WHERE p0 §FRONTIERS ITEM 4 LANDS — stated r1813 by sweep `A5`, because it was named nowhere in
  this file.** *The routing note above says *"the other three route to Lane 4 and Cluster J"* and **Lane 4 carries
  items 1 and 2 by name as `A5.1` and `A5.2`, while item 4 had no statement anywhere** — not under "phase
  structure", not under "everywhere-real", not under its own words.* **Item 4, verbatim: *"The phase structure at
  the seam. The everywhere-real claim is established for the substrate and the seam as a geometric object; the
  hypothesised phase structure at the branch point relative to trajectories is the interpretive layer the conjugacy
  programme opens, its geometric base real and its trajectory/phase reading open. Decide whether it is real
  structure or interpretation. [reach — held do-not-assert, BOTH ways.]"***
  ***So the routing was true at the cluster level and empty at the item level: Cluster J is where it goes, and
  nothing in the plan said what "it" is.*** **Now it does — and the do-not-assert-both-ways register travels with
  it, which is the part that would have been lost.**
**So the ledger's seven families are the open *physics*; Lane 4 holds the *decidable edges of the vision*; and
Cluster J is exploratory. Every advertised door lands somewhere, and the somewhere is named.**

**Clusters with no family:** **C** (structural-completion computations) · **D** (perspectival-reframing) ·
**H** (corpus-wide refinement) · **I** (the P7/P13 split) · **J** (the conjugacy frontier). These are *work*,
not *open problems* — C and H are buildable tasks, I is structural surgery, D and J are exploratory. **That is
why the map has ten clusters and the ledger seven live families: the map tracks everything advertised, the
ledger only what is genuinely unsolved.**

**How to use it.** A result closing or moving a family: update the **ledger** family, then this file's **lane
items** in its row, then the **map cluster**, then the **register rows** for the papers that advertise it — and
the **striking bar** applies throughout: *struck only when fully propagated.* Going the other way, a new
advertised gap enters at the **register**, is judged into a **cluster**, and only becomes a **family** if it
survives the discharge check.

### ⚠ CODE COLLISION BETWEEN GRAINS — found r1559, Phase 1
**`THE_PLAN` and `OPEN_PROBLEMS_MAP` share ten codes, and at least one means two different things.**
- **`D1`** — in `THE_PLAN` it is *"The Big Bang computation"* (**struck**). In the map it is *P2's "do
  FRW-initial / Kerr-inner / RN singularities admit the cyclic removal?"* (**live**, absorbed into the
  geometric-vs-vantage-multiplicity frontier with C5). **Two grains, one code, opposite states.**
- **`A3`** — the plan's Lane-3 quantum completion; the map's *"the sweep diagnostic in non-vacuum."*
- **`A4`** — the plan's matter-content master gate; a differently-scoped map entry.
*(`P1`–`P9` are shared but are paper numbers, not item codes — not collisions.)*

**Why this is a propagation hazard and not a naming quibble.** The crosswalk binds family ↔ frontier ↔ lane ↔
cluster **by code**. **A closure propagated by code alone will strike the wrong item in the other grain** — and
Phase 1 found `D1` in exactly the state that produces: struck in one, live in the other, and a checker that
matched codes would have reported the map as carrying an unpropagated strike. **It is not. It is carrying a
different problem with the same name.**

**Rule: codes are grain-local. Propagate by the CROSSWALK, never by matching a code across documents.**
**This is Phase 1a in the consolidation plan's sequence — an item, not a note.** Disambiguate `D1`, `A3`, `A4`: rename in one grain, or mark the collision at every site in both. **No code-based propagation until it is done.**
**✔ RESOLVED r1579 (Phase 1a):** the map's codes are now `A·1`, `D·1`, `E·3` — *cluster · item* — across 328 occurrences, so they cannot collide with this file's lane codes. Algebra labels (A₂, S₃, D₆) verified untouched.

## The rules that keep it one instrument
1. **Grain discipline.** A finding goes to the grain that fits: a new advertised open → the map; a change in
   what is genuinely unsolved → the ledger; a change in what is blocked → here; a per-paper harvest → the
   weave. **Putting it in the wrong grain is how the copies started.**
2. **Propagate down the chain, not sideways.** The map distils to the ledger, the ledger sorts into lanes.
   Editing a lane without the ledger, or a ledger family without the map's items, is what produced four
   diverging copies of the open problems.
3. **The striking bar** — from the map, and it governs all five: **an item is struck only when its result is
   *fully propagated*** — into the paper's body, intro and abstract; into P7's synthesis, frontiers and
   outlook; and into the meta-docs. *"Strike = genuinely resolved AND fully propagated. Everything short of
   that is annotated, not struck."*
4. **Currency is checked in both directions.** **⚠ And a kill must be propagated to every grain that carries
   it — the sheet-to-ruling item was killed at r1280 and found still live in FIVE documents afterwards:**
   `THE_PLAN`'s Lane 1 (to r1442), the entry-point register's seven rows (to r1498),
   `ANTIMATTER_FRONT_PLAN`'s header (to r1542), and the work packages `WP_C3_SHEET_ASSIGNMENT` and
   `WP_E_ANTIMATTER_NAMING` (to r1548). **One closure, five documents, ~270 revisions.** **A closure that
   reaches one document is not propagated** — and the count is the argument: nobody was careless five times,
   the chain simply had no rule saying propagate *down every grain*. Each carries what revision it is current to. When one is
   updated, the others are checked — the r1471 currency pass found five stale lane items, all stale from a
   single session's work that never came back up the chain.

you change a paper. Sourced from `KICKOFF_ARTHUR.md` r1465; other sources fold in as they are filtered.*

(added r1843).** *It is not tied to turn-start or turn-end. **It fires whenever a sentence touches the substrate,
a cut, a layer or a spacetime** — which on this programme is most sentences. **The five rungs and the words to
use are in the section below and in `ONTOLOGY_FOUNDATION_INDEX` §0.***

**⌖ THE RUNNING ORDER — added r1748, because the sections below are in the order they were WRITTEN, not the
order they FIRE.** *The list grew by accretion across r1465→r1746 and was never read as a list. **Not reordered:
`THE_OPERATING_MANUAL`, `README` and `INDEX` all cite these headings, and a span-reorder of 12 KB is the exact
operation that destroyed eight plan items at r1585.** So the order is stated here and the sections stay where
they are.*

| when | run |
|---|---|
| **turn start** | **§⛭ OPENING A TURN** — the three that fire the operating layer · with **§The mirror** as step 1's method |
| **before a paper edit** | **§Before editing any paper with a précis abstract** — locate by line, not by phrase |
| **while writing** | **§Writing into the corpus** · **§Stating something you cannot yet ground** · **§Two standing guards on CR's own language** |
| **after a paper edit** | **§the masthead check** — and first, print the section the edit landed in |

| **always, while reading** | **§Every turn** — the three-stage hold check · header-against-body **before trusting** · map-navigated, not full-reread · gap tracking |
| **state advance** | **§Closing a state advance** — log · `depmatrix.py` · cut · increment. **Not batchable to end of session** |
| **turn end** | **§Closing the turn** — verified is not delivered · never write "stated for reversal" · do not manufacture a decision point |

is the **method** (*name the act, pull both poles from the field note*). Written 219 revisions apart, r1483 and
