## The dependency graph (what gates what)

```
   [matter-sector CONTENT: A4.1 colour, A4.3 mass-hierarchy, A4.4 forced-link]
        │            │                     │
        ▼            ▼                     ▼
   A2.2 baryo-IC   A6.2 interior      A5.1 free-data-count test
        │           remainder              │
        ▼          A6.4 grand-claim        ▼
   A2.1 BBN                          [maximal-symmetry vision verdict]
   likelihood
        │
   A2.3 peak-heights ──▶ A1.3 likelihood selection ──▶ [cosmology verdict → cosmiCave]
        │
   (partial: the coherence-sufficiency transfer runs ahead of the matter gate)

   A3 quantum completion ◀── downstream of essentially everything (the deepest reach)

   Lane-2 tests (A1.1, A1.4) — ungated: state and hold, wait on the world.
   Lane-5 hygiene, Lane-6 dissemination — ungated, parallel, Daryl-paced.
```

**⚠ THE GRAPH IS WRONG IN TWO EDGES — corrected in prose r1781, the diagram left as the record.**
*① **`A6.4` is shown under the matter-sector content. P12 gates it on the PROPAGATING GRAVITATIONAL SECTOR PAST
THE WALL** — *"the algebroid hands off to them at the wall"* — **which is `A6.1`, and `A6.1` does not appear in
the graph at all.** The missing edge is **`A6.1` → `A6.4`.**
*② **`A6.2` is shown under the same master gate. P7 gates it on the branch-point crossing dynamics of a concrete matter
model — `A2.4`, in Lane 1** — which the crosswalk records correctly as *"gated on family 1"* (r1780).*
***So the master gate unlocks less than the graph says: the baryogenesis IC and the free-data-count verdict, not
the interior remainder and not the grand claim.*** **Two of the four arrows out of it belong elsewhere, and one
of those elsewheres is an item the graph omits.**
**Reading it:** the matter-sector *content* is the master gate — it unlocks the baryogenesis IC and the
free-data-count verdict *(the interior remainder and the grand claim were listed here too; see the correction
above)*. The cosmology-verdict chain (peak-heights → likelihood) is partly runnable now (Lane 1) and completes behind the matter gate. The quantum completion sits below all of it. The Lane-2 decisive tests are ungated — claims to hold, not work to gate. Dissemination (cosmiCave) runs in parallel throughout.

## Companions

**`retired/THE_REFINEMENT_ARC.md` — the phase (opened r1120).** **Its ⌗ WORK QUEUE is the laundry list — one
home, all sweeps, struck as they close.** This plan holds the *route* (lanes, gates, research); the arc
holds the *refinement queue*. A finding there becomes an item here only when it is genuinely research —
the r1120 lesson being that an advertised gap is a **claim to dig**, not a plan item (`A6.6`, routed and
retracted the same turn: a phantom). The programme's current phase: the build
finished, the gaps gathered, and the work now mainly **gap-closing rather than open-wound-uncovering** —
each gap either closed or fulsomely handled and left open **with a sign**. Holds the phase's routine
(how a session runs), its state table (which sweeps are run and owed), and the cold take-over read.
**A node picking this arc up reads it first.**

**`E1_CITATION_CATALOGUE.md` — the reference sweep: opened r1142, ✔ COMPLETE r1144. Now the RECORD, not a
list.** Carries **§THE CRITERION** (Daryl r1143 — *a reference is a pointer to the actual paper, a footnote
on the thing; given for a reason; a person can be named without one; **and the prior leans no***) and the
**per-paper verdicts for all 15 papers**, every strike with its reason. **Read it before touching any
citation in this corpus.** Its three durable findings:
- **The convention test is what finds doors.** All **9** came from *does this paper cite its own peers at
  this role, and is this site the exception?* — **every door was a paper inconsistent with itself, never a
  name appearing.** The **"obvious cases" ranking found 0 of 9 and is demoted in place**: it counts
  *mentions*, and a mention-count sees **strings, not roles**.
- **The ownership rule: the owner determines the importer's verdict, so ask ownership BEFORE the pass.**
  P3-first completed P5's dead-ended chain; P8-first completed P16's. *Importer-first would have struck
  their mentions as "routed" into sections with nowhere to route — a verdict that reads correct and strands
  the reader.*
- **The mechanical gather's blind spots, all found by reading:** `%`-comment mastheads · a publisher's city
  (*"Springer-Verlag, **New York**"*) · an author list inside a bibitem (`MTW1973`) · **a surname inside an
  object's name** (*"**Wheeler**--DeWitt"* → DeWitt's paper; *"**Eddington**--Finkelstein"* → Finkelstein's)
  · **LaTeX accent escapes** (`Painlev\'e` — which hid **two real doors**) · **multi-key `\cite{a,b}`** ·
  **functions named after people** (Bessel ×2). **The prior that pays: the bibitem usually already exists
  in a neighbour — LIFT it, never retype it** (twice this pass the gate began writing one from recognition
  and was saved by an unrelated assertion, not by the guard).

**`ENTRY_POINT_REGISTER.md` — the gap ledger (opened r1120).** Every gap the corpus advertises in its own
words, gathered: **124 sites across 16 of the 17 papers**, where this plan routes roughly a dozen. It is
the register of *where the maze is unexplored and how to walk in* — each entry owed a **sign** (what is
known beyond · the route in · why it matters · the cheapest next step · why it was left open) and a
**discharge condition** (what would close this door, and has it happened). Built by `THE_METHOD` §7-3a.
**Read it before concluding any part of the programme is unmapped, and before opening any frontier —
the door may already be described.**

The retired noise (resolved/superseded items, the stood-down cold-read infrastructure, this-turn's completed work) is recorded reversibly in `RETIRED_PLANNING_THREADS.md`. The method that produced all of this is `THE_METHOD.md`; the raw un-sifted catalogue is `PROGRAMME_UNFINISHEDNESS_CATALOGUE.md`. The **spawn / kickoff apparatus** — how we spin up **Arthur** (Cowork holder) and **Excalibur** (chat workhorses) to work this plan's edge — is built and live: `SPINUP.md` (the index + generic wake-up calls), `KICKOFF_ARTHUR.md`, `KICKOFF_EXCALIBUR.md` (the design record is `PROGRAMME_OVERHAUL_PLAN.md`, executed r927).

# ⛔ NON-NEGOTIABLES — a reference list. **It does not set priority.** (opened r1570; corrected r1574)

**⚠ WHAT THIS IS, AND WHAT IT IS NOT.** *(Daryl, r1574:)* ***"They are just a few items way down the list that
I have identified as non-negotiables. Their status as non-negotiables neither changes where they fall in the
prioritised list nor does it form an exhaustive bar… that status cannot be used to move the bar around
arbitrarily and fuck the whole programme. The bar's placements are structural and depend on other things.
PUBLICATION IS NOT A DRIVER FOR ANY OF THE WORK WE DO."***

- **It is not a gate list.** Everything in the sequence register blocks publication; these are a few named
  items inside that, recorded because they are easy to forget.
- **It is not exhaustive, and it is growing.** More will be named as they are recognised.
- **⚠ IT DOES NOT SET PRIORITY.** *"Non-negotiable" is a statement about the finished thing, not about what to
  do next.* **Sequence is structural** — determined by what verifies what, what dirties what, and what one
  piece of work needs from another. **A node that reorders the docket because an item is marked non-negotiable
  has moved the bar arbitrarily, which is the failure this heading exists to prevent.**
- **Publication drives nothing.** The work is driven by its own structure. *These entries are context for
  understanding why thoroughness matters — not instructions about what to do first.*
